ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u376	Dear Marshal! Dear Members, High Izbo! Of course, at the beginning, I would like to present to the High House the position of the government in relation to the parliamentary draft law amending the Tax Order, print No 1751. The government's position negatively assessed the solutions proposed in the parliamentary draft law amending the Tax Order, amending the article. 77 § 1 point 5 and § 2 of the Act of 29 August 1997 on the reduction from 3 months to 30 days of the refund of the excess tax paid over taxes due, i.e. in income tax, arising from the date of the submission of the tax return, in a special hydrocarbon tax, arising from the date of the submission of the annual declaration, in excise duty, arising from the date of the submission of the excise duty declaration, in payments from profits due from single-member companies of the Treasury and state-owned enterprises, arising from the date of the submission of the declaration for the financial year, and the date on which the excess payment of the tax resulting from the correction of the declaration made by the tax authority pursuant to Article was made. 274 § 1 point 1 of the Tax Order and by the taxpayer, as well as arrangements for the application to pending cases under the draft transitional provision. It was also proposed that the deadline for the refund of the excess tax should remain unchanged, i.e. up to 3 months, and in any case where the tax office, following an analysis of the amount indicated for reimbursement, finds that there are no reasonable grounds for stopping the refund, should make such reimbursement without undue delay. I will present the motives of this position. Currently filed statements or statements with amounts shown to be returned require a number of actions: introduction of data into the system, risk analysis and assessment, frequent checking or tax control activities. A significant proportion of refunds are made immediately, e.g. in the case of PIT-28, i.e. the basic tax return, more than 70% were made reimbursements up to 29 days from the date of submission of the statement or declaration in respect of the total reimbursements thereof. Despite the development of IT in tax offices and the progressive increase in the level of public e-government in Poland still a large number of taxpayers use the paper form of settlement. In connection with the reform of the tax administration, tax officials have been burdened with new tasks, and this task would be the next task that would burden them much more. Shortening the deadline for reimbursement from 3 months to 30 days could therefore lead to a risk of delays and irregularities in handling statements or statements with amounts shown to be refunded and thus a negative assessment of the functioning of tax offices and indirectly of the whole state, resulting in the loss of taxpayers' trust. High Izbo! In view of the arguments put forward in the proposal, a negative position was expressed on the draft law discussed today. I hope that in this government position there was also an answer to most of the questions that have been raised here. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u380	Dear Marshal! Dear Members, High Izbo! I am pleased to present a government bill amending the Tax Order Act, print no. 1836. The government's amendment to the provisions of the Tax Order implies adding to the existing catalogue open as evidence in tax proceedings also documents collected during the analysis activities of the National Tax Administration. The new rules will help to eliminate possible doubts and eliminate taxpayers' denial of the use by tax authorities of documents collected in the framework of analytical activities. The draft law also provides for the exclusion of the application of tax secrecy provisions in respect of cases where the taxable person carrying out an economic activity is made available to the counterparty by means of information on the fulfilment of certain tax obligations by the taxpayer, e.g. concerning the failure or submission by the taxable person of declarations or other documents to which he was required under the provisions of the tax laws. The proposed law also provides for the provision of such information by indicating that the tax authority, at the request of the taxpayer's counterparty, will issue certificates of information on the taxpayer's tax situation, excluding the provisions on tax secrecy, including information on the taxpayer's failure to lodge a declaration despite the expiry of the time limit laid down for its submission. The proposed amendments will help protect taxpayers from the consequences of the dishonesty of their counterparties, aim at creating a procedure for a fair assessment of counterparties and enable the taxpayer to obtain information on the tax situation of the counterparty. The project was added to the article. 181 open catalogue record that the documents collected in the course of analytical activities of the National Tax Administration are also evidence in tax proceedings. It is important that this change does not affect the ongoing proceedings, since under the current rules, anything that may contribute to the clarification of the case should be allowed as evidence and not illegal. The government proposes that the rules enter into force 30 days after the date of publication. In view of the subject matter of the project and the benefits for taxpayers, we support the amendment within that time limit. The proposed law will have a positive impact on the activities of honest entrepreneurs. The introduction of the possibility to check the tax situation of the counterparty will contribute to the creation of business certainty and will serve to eliminate unfair entrepreneurs. ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u398	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Polish People's Party Parliamentary Club, I have the honour to present my position towards the government draft law amending the Tax Order Act, print no. 1836. The bill examined introduces two amendments to the Tax Order Act. One is the provision allowing additional documents in tax proceedings. The entry in the Act of the Open Catalogue is added that the documents collected in the course of the analysis of the National Tax Administration are also evidence in tax proceedings. In view of the article. 180 § 1 of the Act stating that, as evidence, anything which may contribute to the clarification of the case should be allowed, and is not contrary to the law, the provision proposed in the amendment of the Act raises doubts and is incomprehensible. The question is whether the designers want to be considered as a document on some kind of analysis. This should be considered too far-reaching a qualification of the document. The second amendment proposed in the proposal is to exclude the application of the tax confidentiality rules as regards the provision of information on tax returns or other documents submitted by the taxpayer to the counterparty by the taxpayer on the accuracy of those declarations, on the taxpayer's arrears or non-payment in taxes resulting from declarations or other documents submitted under tax laws. Therefore, at the request of the counterparty, the tax authority is required to issue a certificate containing the necessary information. The entity entitled to apply for a certificate may be anyone who is a counterparty to a taxable person established in the business, and the counterparty to the taxpayer may be all entities that are party to a financial transaction which is intended to exchange goods or services. The purpose of this amendment is to protect taxpayers from unfair counterparties, to conduct a procedure for a fair assessment of counterparties and to obtain information on the tax situation. In conclusion, it should be noted that the proposed amendment to the Act, giving the opportunity to verify the tax situation of the counterparty, can contribute to the creation of business certainty and the elimination of unfair entrepreneurs. The Parliamentary Club PSL is in favour of directing the project to further work in Parliament.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u400	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! The Sejm has accelerated the proceedings, so I apologize for having kept you waiting for a while.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u401	We waited, Minister, there's no problem. You're welcome. Minister of Interior and Administration Mariusz Błaszczak: High Izbo! I have the honour to present to the State a draft law amending the Act on certain powers of servants of the office serving the Minister responsible for Home Affairs and officials and servants of the offices supervised by that Minister and certain other laws. This law will also amend the title and become a law on special forms of supervision by the Minister responsible for Home Affairs as from the date of entry into force. High Seym! The new title of the Act fully reflects the intentions we had in our work on this project. The services must act in a transparent manner and the minister must have real and not theoretical supervision over them. This is extremely important, this is the essence of this proposal that I am making to the High House. The draft law is primarily a realistic standard of civil oversight of uniformed public order services. In the current state of law, the Minister of Home Affairs and Administration, despite formal sovereignty over the Police, the Border Guard, the Government Protection Office or the State Fire Department, has in practice significantly limited possibilities for fully autonomous and direct examination of signals of negative phenomena in these services or of undesirable behaviours of their officers. The laws in force governing the activities of services supervised by the Minister responsible for Home Affairs only define the scope and form of supervision exercised by the Minister in selected areas of their operation. These provisions do not create all the supervisory tasks and responsibilities that the Minister of Home Affairs and Administration is required to provide because of the competences and tasks entrusted to him. It is therefore necessary to develop solutions to ensure that supervisory functions can be actually carried out by obtaining up-to-date and structured knowledge of supervised services and candidates for particularly responsible positions, as well as a smooth information flow system and legal instruments allowing the Minister to react quickly and appropriately to specific information and events. The need for mechanisms to strengthen the existing instruments of supervision of the Minister of Home Affairs and Administration also results from the use by supervised services of statutory powers which provide for the possibility of significant interference in civil rights and freedoms, as well as from the performance of tasks liable to cause corruption. One of the most important areas under the supervision of the Minister will therefore be compliance with the standards of operation of the Police, Border Guard, the Government Protection Office and the State Fire Department in connection with the need to ensure respect for civil rights and freedoms and the principles of professional ethics of these formations. The proposed solution eliminates the existing dysfunction by providing the Minister responsible for Home Affairs with instruments for real supervisory impact, as well as preventing irregularities in areas that have not so far been subject to such measures, including, inter alia, those of the Minister responsible for Home Affairs. as regards the implementation of operational and exploratory activities, investigations and disciplinary proceedings, or the recognition and detection of prosecutions of public crimes and tax offences committed by police officers, Border Guard and Government Protection Office and firefighters of the State Fire Department, and the prevention of such offences. This is extremely important, the point is to highlight these competences only with regard to officers and employees of subordinate services, so that there are no misunderstandings, because I have already heard other statements from some of you. That's narrowing it down to the officers only. I stress this once again very strongly. Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! The supervisory activities provided for in the proposed legislation will be carried out through a new supervisory authority, directly subordinate to the Minister of Home Affairs and Administration, i.e. the inspector of internal surveillance, who will carry out his tasks with the help of the Office of Internal Supervision, which is an organisational cell isolated for this purpose from the Ministry of Home Affairs and Administration. The Internal Surveillance Office will therefore not constitute a new service. It's very important, too. The main tasks of the internal surveillance inspector will be to assist the Minister in enforcement activities in accordance with the law and ethics rules, to verify candidates for certain posts, to appoint for higher degrees, in connection with the intended secondment to serve or perform tasks outside the country for a period of more than 14 days, to award, at the request of the Minister, to award, at the request of the Minister, to disclose and analyse irregularities arising in connection with the investigation and disciplinary proceedings carried out, to examine information relating to infringements of the law by supervised entities, to analyse and evaluate operational and inspection activities carried out in the Police and Border Guard, to the extent that this does not prejudice the powers of the prosecutor and the court, to the recognition and detection of offences committed by police officers, the Border Guard, the Office for the Protection of the Government and firefighters of the State Fire Department, and to the personnel employed in these areas, to the extent that this does not affect the powers of the prosecution and the tax authorities, which requires the authorities to exercise their powers to carry out such functions, to ensure that they are competent to carry out administrative agents and to carry out administrative duties in the performance of officials. The specific nature of the tasks of the internal supervisory inspector determines the separation of this function from the body to which it is subject, namely the Minister for Home Affairs and Administration. At the same time, in order to ensure the effectiveness of the Minister's supervision and to enable him to take the necessary action immediately, the inspector of internal surveillance will be required to provide information and material obtained as a result of the performance of tasks which may be of major importance for the supervision exercised by the Minister. In turn, the Minister will be able to familiarise himself with the results of the operational and exploratory activities, as well as other activities carried out by the inspector of internal surveillance, and at any time to request information and material from him on the performance of the tasks carried out by the Office. I would also like to stress that the functioning of the Internal Surveillance Office in the field of operational and exploratory activities will be subject to identical formal requirements and control mechanisms, as in the case of services with such powers, including judicial review. This solution therefore meets the Polish constitutional standard requiring independent authorities to provide control and supervisory instruments. The primary area in which operational and research activities will be carried out will be crimes committed by service officers supervised by the Minister of Home Affairs, which seriously undermine public confidence in the institutions of the State. Operational control was also allowed in other cases, e.g. suspected involvement of officers in organised crime groups. On the other hand, the aim will be to detect and prevent offences committed by officials and servants under the authority of the Minister for Home Affairs and Administration. Ladies and gentlemen, in addition to the internal surveillance officer and his alternates, Members of the Office will be seconded to carry out the tasks of Police and Border Guard officers, i.e. 35 inspectors of the Office, and they will be assisted by seconded officials of the Government Protection Office and firefighters of the State Fire Department, i.e. 5 experts of the Office, and employed staff, 8 employees. The essence of the proposed solution is based on the knowledge acquired by seconded police officers, Border Guard and Government Protection Office and firefighters of the State Fire Department in the internal functioning of the Home Service, which is intended to facilitate the achievement of the objectives set before the inspector of internal surveillance. In view of the need to ensure an adequate level of preparation and personnel standards, high formal criteria have been set before candidates for internal surveillance posts and their alternates. Equally high requirements have been placed before inspectors and experts of the Internal Surveillance Office, whose activities, due to the specific tasks of the Office, apart from the highest level of professionalism, must also give the guarantee of indisputability and reliability. Inspectors and experts of the Office will therefore not be able, inter alia, to do so. undertake, as a general rule, additional economic activities and be members of a political party, and membership of national, foreign or international organisations or associations will require the written consent of the inspector of internal surveillance. They will also be required to notify the inspector of the planned foreign departure outside the European Union. Inspectors and experts of the Office will also follow the model of the home services to make statements on their assets. Ladies and gentlemen! The proposed law also takes into account the need for a maximum reduction in the financial consequences of the Office's operation for the state budget. This purpose is served, among others. resignation from the maintenance of an extensive staff structure to fill the personal status of the cell with officers seconded to carry out tasks as police and border guards inspectors and experts from the Office for Government Protection and the State Fire Guard. The draft law also provides for amendments to pragmatic laws in the field of rules on the deployment of police officers, Border Guard officers and the Government Protection Office and firefighters of the State Fire Department. These amendments aim at ordering the rules on secondments and making them rationally harmonised, inter alia. the definition of a unitary service for all the services of the circle of entities to which secondment may take place. In addition, regulations which are essential for the rights and obligations of officials are transferred from the implementing acts to the statutory level. The laws of pragmatic services supervised by the Minister responsible for Home Affairs also include the obligation to cooperate with the Office of Internal Surveillance in carrying out its tasks. The proposed legislation fills the existing gap, which is the lack of cells performing similar tasks at the Government Protection Office and the State Fire Department. This lack is due to the limited statutory competence of both services, which do not provide for the powers to undertake the operational and exploratory activities necessary in the process of the disclosure of irregularities, including offences which may occur in the series of those formations. The proposed rules assume that existing internal affairs cells, namely the Office of Internal Affairs of the Police Headquarters and the Office of the Border Guard's Internal Affairs, will be organisationally separated from the main command structures and become independent organisational units. In the case of the Police, an internal service will be isolated, which is a necessary procedure, taking into account the specificities of the operation of the Police Unit, the need to create a separate training model, separate supervisory mechanisms, as well as to ensure independence from other organisational structures as a guarantee of reliability and objectivity. The heads of new units will be replaced by chiefs appointed by the Minister for Home Affairs. The nature of the tasks of the new units will remain unchanged. The proposed organisational solution will undoubtedly increase the efficiency of the existing police and border guards' organisational units responsible for preventing and detecting crimes committed by police officers or Border Guards respectively. The new formula for the operation of the BSW, i.e. the Office for the Interior of the Police and the Office for the Home Affairs of the Border Guard, will also provide greater autonomy for the internal service in carrying out its tasks, which should, as a result, also have a positive impact on its effectiveness. The Police Office and the Border Guard Office's internal affairs officers will at the same time gain the status of authorities, and thus similar powers as with the Central Police Bureau of Investigation and the Police Provincial Commandants with regard to operational control, secretly controlled shipment, purchase of controlled data, obtaining data. Until now, office privileges as organizational cells of the main commands have been implemented through the main commandants. Regulations, written consents and requests in this respect will be communicated to the Home Office of the Police and the Home Office of the Border Guard for information by the inspector of internal surveillance. Financial and logistical support of newly created organisational units, i.e. The Police Office and the Border Guard Office will be conducted by the Police Headquarters and the Border Guard Headquarters respectively, which means that the separation of new organisational units and their operation should not cause additional costs. At the same time, the scope of supervision exercised by the Minister responsible for Home Affairs will be extended to supervise the implementation by the Office of Internal Affairs of the Police and the Office of Home Affairs of the Border Guard for the recognition and detection of and prevention of criminal offences committed by police officers and Border Guards and by public prosecutions and tax offences. In addition, the Chiefs of the Police Office and the Border Guard's Internal Affairs Office will be required to provide the Minister responsible for Home Affairs with information and material which may be relevant for the exercise of this supervision without delay. In turn, the Minister responsible for Home Affairs will at any time be able to request information and material from the Chiefs of the Office of Home Affairs of the Police and the Office of Home Affairs of the Border Guard. The Chiefs of the Police Office and the Office of the Interior will also present annually, by 31 January, the Minister responsible for Home Affairs, information on the activities of the Police Office and the Border Guard Office. High Izbo! In conclusion, I reiterate that the solutions proposed in the proposal presented to the High House, on the one hand, implement the supervision exercised by the Minister responsible for Home Affairs over subordinate services, and, on the other hand, allow for effective and objective verification of all information about irregularities and functioning, as well as the immediate taking of appropriate supervisory action. In the longer term, however, they will create a positive model of an officer whose highest ethical standards should be the basis for public confidence in state institutions. Upon the entry into force of the proposed solutions, an increase in activity should also be assumed. whistleblowers and others interested in eliminating negative phenomena from public service activities, looking for opportunities to investigate reported cases by an independent body. [[Signals.]] Yeah, signalers. [[You mean snitch.]] You're the one with the dictionary. Signals. In the west of Europe, this is a very important function that allows us to actually eliminate abuse. As I understand it, your statements show, you are not interested in eliminating abuse, and you are therefore doing what you are doing. According to the article. Article 34 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 2 of the Act of 8 August 1996 on the Council of Ministers, the Minister directs, supervises and controls the activities of subordinate bodies, offices and bodies. The problem is that the provisions cited above do not provide instruments that would allow for a real diagnosis of the situation in supervised entities and would also allow for a fully effective countering of negative phenomena. These instruments exist, but they remain only at the disposal of certain central government bodies under the authority of the Minister. These instruments are owned by police chiefs and border guards. The tasks of these bodies shall be carried out by means of internal affairs offices. The problem is that the Minister has mostly information processed by the said authorities without the possibility of autonomous and direct action. The essence of this project is to give the Minister of Home Affairs and Administration powers comparable to those of the bodies under his authority. The current legal status with supervisory instruments includes the Police and Border Guard, leaving the Government Protection Office and the State Fire Department out of control. The proposed project creates the possibility for the newly established cell directly subordinate to the Minister, but does not provide for such an obligation, so the Office of Internal Supervision would deal with specific issues which undermine public confidence in the institutions of the State. With regard to the aforementioned internal affairs offices, the headquarters of the Police and Border Guard, they will become organisational units called internal affairs offices of the Police and Border Guard, but the nature of the tasks of the new units will remain unchanged. It is worth noting the problem of the current legal situation, where the minister supervising the service can only receive processed materials. The proposed bill eliminates the risk of subjectivising the selection and assessment of the information provided. ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u428	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present to you a draft law adopted at the Council of Ministers, a law on the financing of educational tasks. This is a project, ladies and gentlemen, which has been developed for many months by a team set up in November Ub. (a) at the Minister of Education, which consisted of experts from the Ministry of Education, representatives of local governments and trade unions. At the same time, it was the result of meetings of experts from the Ministry of Education with local experts who are involved in the financing of education, as well as research by the Institute of Educational Research and reports by the Supreme Audit Chamber. I do not hide that this law was intended to have a much wider scope and there were much deeper changes planned, namely that we wanted education subsidies to look completely different. The one who is illegible, unjust, archaic, hard to count. First of all, we wanted to make this educational subsidy dependent on the branch, not only on the students, but at the request of the local government, we put this off, we completed a stage and we will be going back to the end of November to further analyze just such treatment of the educational subsidy, so that in 2019 we can propose solutions to which the local government, trade unions and the Ministry of Education will agree. Therefore, please, that we have made such a decision, we have also agreed with Parliament that at the end of our term we will part with the Law on the Education System, so we are consistently drawing out individual elements so that laws are made dedicated to very specific components which are in the education system. Hence, we have limited ourselves to what local governments have been waiting for for for many years, i.e. to seal, organize grants. In the Act we have four grants: a textbook grant, a pre-school grant, a scholarship grant, as we call it, and, of course, a grant for non-public institutions or public bodies run by other bodies than local authorities. As far as the textbook grant is concerned, of course this is The effect of the fact that local governments, principals, teachers complain that she is very bureaucratic and very necessary, because all children from the first grade of primary school to the last converted junior high schools receive textbooks for free. It's a large amount in the grant, a large amount in the budget. But such bureaucracy did not allow flexibility in the selection of individual textbooks. At the same time, many organizations and associations emphasized that this might be an excuse to suspect teachers that they support some lobbying of individual publications, especially when it turned out that they were choosing from some publishing house, e.g. The math textbook, they actually choose the whole package because it was more convenient to settle. Therefore, at the request of local governments, we have made such records that say that here is the amount for the school, the principal has this amount in any way. Where you have to pay more for textbooks, you will pay, where you can negotiate and lower the price, you will be able to do so. He's gonna settle the whole grant. At the same time, we have changed that 1% will be allocated to the settlement of this grant. not from the amount spent, but from the grant. I think it's also interesting for the directors, because they actually have a lot of work to do with the grant settlement. Another one, ladies and gentlemen, is a preschool grant. Here was a big problem, there were many misunderstandings between municipalities, the municipalities discussed who was to pay a grant or pay a grant for a kindergarten which was located in one municipality, and the host body or body that decided on the network was another municipality. We're organizing the situation, i.e. This grant will not depend on the location of the facility, but on the operator and the registrant. As far as scholarship grants are concerned, I think it is a great shame for all of us to see it when some municipalities returned social scholarships to the state budget. The reason was brutally simple, namely the fact that in each municipality, regardless of the income of that municipality, participation of 20% had to take place in order to pay a social scholarship. This led to the fact that many municipalities were simply not involved. It's a pity for the money, it's a pity for children who simply deserve these scholarships. We have therefore decided - also at the request of local authorities - that this grant will depend on the wealth of the municipality. Where the municipality is poorer, the state has a specific algorithm shown in the explanatory memorandum and in the law, it will be able to propose a proportionally smaller contribution. I think it's for the good of the children and also for our comfort that we won't be ashamed to admit that, among other things. Such grants return to the state budget. As regards grants for non-public or public establishments run by entities other than the local government, please, there are a lot of changes. They are all fulfilling the needs of local government officials, from such very troublesome ones, which caused many conflicts, including court proceedings. A definition of an increasing indicator will be introduced. This is necessary in the case of subsidised public schools to increase accordingly the amount for the pupil allocated to the education subsidy for the local government unit. The finalisation of social assistance benefits is another element introduced in this law. This is a very important change, because the amount of the scholarship to the student will no longer depend on the wealth of his community. So far, the amount of social aid was dependent on the contribution of the municipalities. The municipalities did not have the same percentage contribution as the wealthy ones. ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u504	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Free and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the government draft law on the financing of educational tasks. The aim of the proposed law is to create a coherent set of rules on the financing of educational tasks. In the current legislation, we have often encountered interpretation problems and many ambiguities. I find the need to amend the rules undisputed. The bill provides for the introduction of e.g. clear rules on the calculation of grants from the state budget for the financing of pre-school education tasks for local government units and simplification of the way grants are cleared, as well as changes in the financing of free textbooks, educational materials and training. In addition, part of the grants in adult schools are planned to be dependent on passing exams and vocational examinations. The grant is to be paid for the actual number of pupils. If a student uses two different schools of preschool education or adult education, only one of these schools will receive a grant. It is important to change the financing of material social benefits, to make the contribution of the municipality in this respect dependent on its financial condition - municipalities with low income ratios, would have to add 5% , and municipalities with an average income ratio of 10% instead of the current 20%. The reduction of the aid from the municipalities' own resources will increase access to this form of support and is therefore worthy of support. In view of demographic and economic changes, the regulations in force in the Act of 26 January 1982 are obsolete and do not conform to present times. I think it is right to update the rules that will respond to the challenges that teachers face now. The draft law provides for, among others, changes in teachers' leave, promotion and evaluation of their work, working time, as well as changes in salaries. With regard to changes in the career promotion path, it should be stressed that extending the promotion time from 10 years to 15 will improve the competences of teachers. In my opinion, it is also a good solution proposed by the projectors to replace the interview with an intern with an examination carried out by an examination panel, which will consist of persons from outside the primary school environment, e.g. representatives of pedagogical supervisors. The introduction of a new salary allowance for outstanding work in my opinion will undoubtedly affect teachers who have achieved the highest professional degree and, most importantly, will raise the level of teaching. Introduced by the proposed mandatory law, cyclical evaluation of the teacher's work in my opinion will be reflected in the quality of the work rendered. It will also have a positive impact on cost rationalisation. I realize, of course, that not all changes are perfect and certainly not everyone will respond. However, we cannot tolerate old, bad solutions. About Free and Solidarni will vote in favour of further proceding the bill.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u510	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! Minister! I have the honour to present the position of the Civic Platform Parliamentary Club regarding the Senate resolution on the Act amending the law on judicial bailiffs and executions. After considering the law adopted by the Sejm, the Senate introduced three amendments to its text. In Amendment 1, the Senate clarified that the time limit set by the bailiff to the debtor for voluntary performance of the obligation may not be less than 14 days. This amendment is correct, as cases mainly involve evictions, which require time for their execution, and the debtor must have that time guaranteed. It is also necessary to agree with the amendment concerning the setting of a lower fee in the event of the debtor's voluntary performance of the obligation after the specified deadline, but not later than 3 days before the planned execution. In the third amendment, the Senate proposed solutions to simplify settlements in the event of the creditor's payment of a fixed fee in line with the current rules. In conclusion, the Civic Platform Parliamentary Club supports the amendments made by the Senate and will vote in favour of them. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u514	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present on behalf of the club of the Polish People's Party our position on the report of the Senate committee on the bill amending the law on bailiffs and executions. This project is the implementation of the judgment of the Constitutional Court of 2015 on the rate of the flat emptying fee. In its judgment, the Constitutional Tribunal held that one of the provisions of the 1997 Act is non-constitutional in so far as it does not provide for a separate fixed fee for the removal of the dwelling from the property and persons in a situation where the debtor, after being called by the bailiff, has voluntarily fulfilled his obligation within the prescribed time limit. Having regard to the abovementioned ruling of the Constitutional Tribunal, the authors of the draft proposal propose to introduce a temporary fee to the institution against the fee payable for the execution of non-monetary benefits and the execution of the provision for the provision to provide collateral for non-monetary claims and to amend the wording of those provisions which relate to the amount of fees incurred in connection with the execution of the collection of property, the introduction of property and the removal of movable property from it, the introduction of the manager to the management board of the property or of the undertaking and the introduction of the caretaker into the supervision of the property, the emptying of the premises of the property or persons or, finally, the introduction of the creditor into possession in other cases than those mentioned above. After the amendment, unlike today, the initiation of the enforcement of non-monetary benefits will not be subject to payment by the creditor of the full fixed fee, but to a provisional fee of 10%. average monthly salary. At the same time, the final fixed fees charged for the enforcement of non-monetary benefits are to be reduced to 20% average monthly salary for execution of pick-up (currently 50% - Art. 50 U.K.S.E.) and execution of the introduction of real estate and removal of movable property, the introduction of the manager to the management of the real estate or the undertaking, or the introduction of the caretaker into the supervision of the property and the removal of the premises from the property or persons (today it is 40% ) and up to 15% respectively in other cases where it is necessary to bring the creditor into possession. Thus, the fees described are intended to make applicants more adequate in the context of the current socio-economic situation. In doing so, it was proposed that the fees due should be reduced up to 1/25 of the average monthly salary or that the obligation to cover them in full should the debtor decide to carry out the obligation voluntarily (depending on whether he will do so after the bailiff has called on him to comply with the implementing title or before he has been served with such a call). In such a situation, the bailiff will be required to repay the appropriate amount to the creditor. A number of amendments have recently been tabled to the Senate project. The House took the view that a lower charge on the debtor (in fact, a 10% interim charge) the average monthly salary) shall be reasonable provided that the obligation established by the implementing title is fulfilled no later than 3 days before the planned execution. It is worth mentioning that a provision similar to that referred to here, which constitutes the matter of Amendment 2, can be found in Article. 38 of the proposed bill on bail-out costs, Parliamentary printing No. 1581. It is further specified on the basis of the following article. 49b. 3 that the time limit which the bailiff assigns to the debtor for the voluntary performance of the obligation may not be less than 14 days. The introduction of such a reservation should prevent the practice of establishing several days' time limits, i.e. unrealistic, while the mere execution of bailiffs usually plan after a period of several months. Another of the amendments proposed by the Senate aims at simplifying the accounts in the event of the creditor's payment of the fixed fee in accordance with the current rules. In the view of the Senate, where by 22 October 2015 (date of notification of the judgment of 13 October 2015), a final decision on the costs of execution was not implemented and within the time limit laid down in Article. 2 (2) 5 The law will be requested to determine these costs taking into account the availability of the article. 49b. 3, the bailiff should return to the creditor the difference between the fixed fee paid by him and the fee payable under the new rules and, if the debtor has already returned the equivalent of the fixed fee or part of it to the creditor, the relevant settlement should take place with the debtor. High Izbo, this project, along with the Senate's amendments, deserves support. Today, the need to redefine the issue of bail-in fees is evident. The project responds to social expectations in this area. In our view, Senator Lydia Staroń's efforts supported by the two Houses of Parliament should find a realization so that there is less social controversy in our country with bailiffs.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u547	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! In the autumn of 1867 Chojnice was visited by the first nuns of the Franciscan Sisters' Assembly from the Atonement and Christian Love of the Assembly founded by Katarzyna Damen in 1835 in the Netherlands. Chojnice is the first Polish city under partitions, where the Franciscans found their place of work and prayer. It's been 150 years since then. It's a beautiful anniversary of the service of the Christian nuns in my town. Since the beginning of their activities in Chojnice, the Franciscans have been very involved in educational and caring activities. They ran a kindergarten, elementary school and orphanage, including the whole Chojnic community: Catholics, Protestants and Jews. This social activity was the salt in the eye of the Prussian possessor, who in every possible way hindered the functioning of the assembly. The apogee of the repressive action of kulturkampfu caused that in August 1875 the sisters had to leave Chojnice. Thanks to the efforts of Fr. Reverend Augustine Behrendt, director of the Zamartem Fr. Dr. Anthony Wolszlagier and Prefect Fr. After 10 years in November 1885, Dr. Klemens Ludtke returned to Chojnice. The sisters returned to St. Boromeus, who was a nun's house. During the absence of the sisters in Chojnice, thanks to the efforts of the local Catholic community at the plant, the construction of the hospital began. The return of the nuns to Chojnice was a great joy for the community of the city. The residents decorated the city, the parish church and St. Boromeus. These gestures and generosity of the Chojniczans, who supported the sisters who did not receive any wages, bear witness to the great gratitude of the inhabitants for the work they did for the Chojniczans. After returning to Chojnice, the sisters participated very actively in the construction of the hospital: cleaning, washing, scrubbing and cleaning the debris from the building. In the second half of 1886 the first patients were admitted to the hospital. The nurses and the roomkeepers were Franciscan sisters. The sisters continued their educational and caring activities in kindergarten and orphanage. A very difficult experience for the Order and sisters was the time of World War I and II when they were forced to care for soldiers of the criminal German system and care for them. The Franciscans risked their lives to help and care for the guerrillas of Gryf Pomorskie and the sick Poles. A very painful and tragic experience for nuns was the entry of the Red Army into Chojnice. After the march of 15 February 1945, Soviet soldiers murdered Sister Adelgund Tuminska, who stood up for the girls who were attacked. The times of the greatest Stalinist oppression and the fight against the church in Poland the Franciscans in Chojnice survived thanks to the mother of the superior Rafaela Schmidt. The Communist authorities systematically removed the sisters from their positions in the hospital and kindergarten. In 1949, the State requisitioned the Holy House. Boromeus and the Holy House Joseph, and in 1961 the Marian house, and in 1981. The house of St. Francis. The summit of communist persecutions was the idea of moving the sisters to the barracks at the town slaughterhouse. Fortunately, it was not realized. Until 1948. Chojnice was the main home of the sisters in Poland. In the autumn of 1948, after the growing chicans of power, the function of the main house was transferred to Orlik. The Franciscan sisters have been inseparable from the history of Chojnice for 150 years. From this point on, I speak heartfeltly. God pay for the work that the sisters did and do all the time for Chojnice residents, for sacrifice, prayer, catechesis, service to the parishes. On the eve of the anniversary of the 150th anniversary of my arrival in Chojnice, I wish my sisters the best wishes of God's blessing every day of your ministry. God bless you! Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u549	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! On 23 September 2017 it was an extremely important day for the community of School Complex No. 3 in Ostrowiec Świętokrzyskie, a facility which, in the consciousness of many generations of inhabitants of the city, registered as the Hutniczko-Mechaniczne Technikum, or popular THM. The history of this facility began with the request made in June 1921 by the strikers of the Ostrowice Works, who asked the directorate to set up craft courses for both younger and older workers in order to complement and improve their professional qualifications. This postulate was completed and in 1922 at the Ostrowiec University of Technology, such courses were organized, and the decision of the Ministry of Religious Confession and Public Enlightenment was legalized as a vocational school, one of three then operating in Ostrowiec. Thus the year 2017 is celebrated as the jubilee year, the year of the 95th anniversary of the school. In less than 100 years, the facility operated under eight names, and the organs that carried it changed. However, it must be said that perfect teachers and students were a constant feature of this school. She was also lucky to have excellent executives. Of the eight directors of this institution, I would like to mention Leon Chrzanowski, Sławomir Kopański, an excellent mathematician, Member of the Sejm in 1989 and head of the school today Czesław Golis. The brand of the School Complex No 3 was and is a high level of teaching vocational subjects as well as general education. This is proved by the numerous successes of the students in the Olympics and competitions concerned and by the high places in the rankings of the best technical schools. In the ranking of the Polish Rzeczpospolita and the Perspektywwy between 2011 and 2014 this school was considered the best technical school of the Świętokrzyskie voivodship. Ladies and gentlemen! This great anniversary is an excellent opportunity to showcase the great achievements of the school over the past decades. Unique educational achievements, a number of outstanding graduates who have made the name of the popular THM in Poland and the world famous, and thousands of well-educated specialists are the result of the work of all those who for years have educated and raised the next generations of young people in our city and voivodship. Today, therefore, I would like to express my deepest appreciation and respect for the next directors, teachers and all the employees who have shared their knowledge and skills with the students for nearly a century, and all those who today have so wonderfully continued their work. Appreciating your daily efforts to carry out your duties conscientiously and creatively and congratulating you on your achievements, I wish you as much satisfaction from your work as possible. I congratulate the students of the School Complex No 3 on the choice of such a distinguished institution and wish you enthusiasm and perseverance in gaining knowledge. The school whose students you are gives you great opportunities for development. Try to make the most of it, and then the next years will bring even more success and cause for joy. I wish this whole school community with all my heart. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u550	Thank you very much. Mr Krzysztof Maciejewski, Law and Justice have the floor. There's no envoy. In that case, please, Mr Waldemar Andzel, also the Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u551	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! This year we celebrate the 75th anniversary of the creation of the National Armed Forces, a conspiracy organization of independence. The National Armed Forces were officially established on 20 September 1942, in fact the process of forming the NSZ began in July 1942 by order of colonel. Ignacy Oziewicz, first commander of the main formation. The National Armed Forces are one of the most famous organizations of Poland's Underground War and Postwar period. Next to the Home Army and the Battalions of the peasants, they were the largest political-military formation during the occupation. At the time of joining the NSZ with the National Army in March 1944, the number of soldiers included in the NSZ was around 90,000. The National Political Council has been the governing body of the NSZ temporarily. NSZ was an organization created by a large number of professional pre-war officers. The organization was to cover Poland before 1939, as well as areas inhabited by Poles located before the war in German territory. Six inspectors were created in these areas, divided into 17 districts. The main programming objectives of the NSZ were formulated in the Declaration of the National Armed Forces of February 1943. They wanted to fight against Hitler's Germans as well as the Soviet Union, recognizing the Soviets as the greatest threat. They sought full sovereignty and reconstruction of Polish statehood. Nevertheless, NSZ troops were often unfairly accused of collaborating with Germany. They were unjustly attributed to the crimes committed during the war on the Jewish people, whereas many Jews served in the National Armed Forces divisions, and they were given the necessary support and assistance. Soldiers of the National Armed Forces also bravely fought in the Warsaw Uprising. The intelligence network, which operated in the territory of the Third Reich, has placed special merits. The National Armed Forces also dealt with propaganda activities, published about 100 titles, books on political or legal issues, and even training manuals for military verticals. In the publications of the NSZ one could find speeches of the Holy Father Pius XII, also to the Polish nation. The achievements and courage of the soldiers of the National Armed Forces have been ignored for years by silence, and in the end they have even been forgotten. Therefore, celebrations of the anniversary of the uprising of the NSZ are so important to restore their rightful place in the history of our nation. It should be remembered that NSZ was a national organization entirely free of foreign influence, ideologically independent and financially independent from the fighting parties. They fought not only for the independence of Poland. They had a political concept, had their own economic and educational programme. This is what distinguishes them from other organizations of this type. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u554	Thank you. Mrs Zofia Czernow, Civic Platform, has the floor.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-09-28-sejm-48-2.u555	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! My statement is devoted to the 800th anniversary of the Silesian Lviv. Lwówek Śląski belongs to the group of the oldest cities in Lower Silesia. A rich story encourages us to discover his secrets and compels us to ponder over the past power of this castle. It is located in a place where spruce forests slowly begin to yield to pine sites, and the River Beaver with its picturesque current separates the Kaczawskie Pogórze from the Jizera Foothills. Eight centuries ago, the Piast castle of Lviv Śląski was established here. The first settlers began to settle in the area of today's city in the late bronze age, but in fact the town of Lviv and surrounding villages began to grow only in the 12th century, when gold began to be mined in the area of the present city. The wealth of precious stones and their great interest in exploitation contributed to the rapid growth of the settlement. The second important factor in increasing the importance of the castle was the location on the main trade route leading from western Europe to the Halicka Rus. In 1209 the castle received numerous privileges from Prince Henryk Brodaty, among others. the law of brewing beer, milling flour and hunting within a radius of 1 mile from the settlement. Since the uprising, the city has been one of the most famous castles in Lower Silesia. The material symbol is the urban fortification team, which belong to the most interesting of its kind in Lower Silesia, create an exceptionally rich assumption with two defence circuits. The lion's fortifications quickly reached a level to deter even the strongest enemies, such as the Czech Hussites. The granting of urban rights in 1217 confirmed the privileges already acquired and involved the granting of new customs duties. The city quickly gained the position of one of the larger castles in Lower Silesia. At the end of the 14th century, Lviv was almost 13 thousand. The inhabitants and wealth only gave way to Wroclaw. Around Lwówek Śląski, numerous villages were created, which served as a supply for the city. Historical materials confirm the 800-year history of towns such as Zbylutów, Chmielno or Skorzynice. The late Middle Ages brought about the development of craftsmanship, especially the characteristic of the gathering of dresses. A huge blow to the city was the Thirty Years' War. Then Lwówek was completely robbed - first by Polish troops and later by the Austrian emperor's troops. Another great cataclysm occurred in 1813, when the surrounding areas were devastated by Napoleonic troops being hunted down by the Russian army. The Emperor Napoleon himself stayed in Lviv for 3 days in 1913 and personally commanded the battle over the Beaver, in which the troops of the great army replaced the Prussian troops from the village of Płakowice. The 19th century was a period of lush development for Lviv. New industrial plants were built, palaces were built and old ones renovated, unique parks, greenery were established. Then Mr Julius Hochberg began to build a modern brewery, which is still in operation today. The period of prosperity was interrupted by the crisis that affected the entire German lands after the end of World War I. Since then, Lviv Silesia has never rebuilt its position again. After the end of World War II, the city was located within the borders of the Polish state. The settlers from the whole pre-war area of Poland came here. The city became a regional industrial and administrative centre. It currently counts about 10,000. residents, is the seat of the authorities of the commune and the district of Lwówecki. Extremely beautiful architectural monuments, charming streets, rich history, as well as picturesque landscapes and panoramic views of Sudeta, the Krkonoše Mountains attract crowds of tourists. There are treasure hunters here, because the surroundings of Lwówek Śląski are famous for the known in Europe agate and sandstone deposits and centuries-old mining traditions. On the occasion of the anniversary of the 800th anniversary of granting city rights, I congratulate the inhabitants of Lviv Śląski with warm congratulations and wishes for further success and every success. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]
